| | * commit 65f4cf19d7a084d65a583186373a4448940ae0f6
| | | Author: Bảo Ngọc Hà <hiembaonon@gmail.com>
| | | Date:   Sun Jul 26 21:56:19 2026 +0700
| | | 
| | |     fix(usability): force the search step, don't just imply it
| | |     
| | |     Previous wording ("find 1 product you like, under X price") let
| | |     participants complete the whole task by clicking straight from the
| | |     5-product grid, never touching the search box — the "search by
| | |     keyword" step of the flow was never actually required to happen.
| | |     
| | |     Rewrote each task to assign a specific keyword the participant must
| | |     type into the search box (verified live: each returns exactly 1
| | |     match). Keywords vary in form across participants (full name, partial
| | |     name, Vietnamese diacritics, uppercase) to exercise different search
| | |     behaviors while keeping per-participant task difficulty identical.
| | |

| | |     All 7 price thresholds (300,000₫-1,000,000₫) were far below the SUT's
| | |     actual cheapest product (Keychron Q1, 4,000,000₫) — verified live at
| | |     localhost:5173. No product could ever match, so the "search by
| | |     keyword" step of the flow would always return 0 results and no
| | |     participant could complete the task.
| | |     
| | |     Rewrote thresholds against the real catalog (4tr/6tr/28tr/30tr/45tr),
| | |     each yielding a different valid product set (1 product up to all 5)
| | |     to keep the per-participant variation the design already called for.

| | |     docs: reframe evidence as black-box observations, not repo source reads
| | |     
| | |     HW03 Task 1 is checklist-based black-box GUI testing. Several bug
| | |     reports and checklist Notes cited local repo file paths/line numbers
| | |     (ProductDetail.jsx, Home.jsx, CartContext.jsx, backend/server.js) as
| | |     proof, which misrepresents the testing methodology as white-box.
| | |     
| | |     Rewrites every such citation as a pure black-box observation instead
| | |     — behavior reproduced across repeated trials, DevTools/Inspect
| | |     Element, Network tab, console — while keeping the underlying defect
| | |     claims intact and, in most cases, on stronger footing (e.g. the SQL
| | |     injection finding now rests on two independent black-box techniques,
| | |     error-based and boolean-based, exactly as a real pentester without
| | |     source access would establish it).
| | |     
| | |     No defect conclusions changed — only how the evidence is presented.

| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-002 to ensure API `/api/admin/*` rejects requests without a token.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-003 to check that invalid tokens are rejected for API `/api/admin/*`.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-004 to confirm that regular user tokens are not accepted for admin API access.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-005 to validate that admin users can access the API `/api/admin/*`.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-006 to ensure data writing is rejected without a token.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-007 to verify that invalid tokens are rejected for data writing APIs.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-008 to check that regular users cannot write data for coupons.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-009 to confirm that admin users can write data for products/categories/coupons.
| |       - Created TC-FR12-DT-010 to ensure public APIs do not require admin rights.
| |       - Added pairwise test cases TC-FR12-PW-001 to TC-FR12-PW-007 to cover various scenarios for product and category operations with different token states.
